# AlgebraicGeometry.ProjectiveSpace.isConnectedCurve_smooth_proper_genus_zero

## Reason

Repair the return of delivery 4e8b4e577f7: its base treated the item as absent, but
sheaf-dag restored it while that delivery was pending. This packet starts from the
current live source, not the quarantine archive. The current source already contains
the ProjectiveSpace public import proposed by the first packet. Preserve that fix,
place public imports before private proof imports, and explicitly publicly import
WeilConjectures.Topology.RingedSpaceModuleCohomology, which defines
RingedSpace.moduleCohomology in the last clause of the public theorem.
This makes that statement dependency explicit rather than relying on a transitive
public path through Curve. It is an additive public-import repair, not a rebase-only
or unchanged re-push.
